Let's design a new algorithm: "Active-coefficient multiplicative mirror descent with annealed target and coordinate polish".

Detailed functions:
- `_fft_score(p, L=None)`: p np array sum1 nonnegative. compute F=rfft(p,L), c=irfft(F*F,L)[:2n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0). score=2*n*c.max(). Return score,c.
- `_active_weights(c, target, beta, use_thresh, eps)`:
  If maxc > target: weights proportional to (c - (1-eps)*maxc + eps*target)? We can use threshold at tau = max(target, maxc*(1 - slack) + slack*target) maybe. We want focus on high coefficients. Else softmax(beta*c).
  Need ensure weight sums 1? The gradient step scale can be normalized; direction matters. We can normalize h to max 1 or mean. Previous didn't normalize except h/hm. We can normalize `h = h/hm` to make α dimensionless. Good.
- `_mirror_step(p, h, alphas)`: normalize h max1; for α in grid, p2=p*exp(-α h), floor, normalize. Evaluate score. Some α might increase. Choose best. Also maybe use additive? Multiplicative keeps positivity.
- To avoid all coordinates changing too much, cap h range and α.
- We can also use "dual-residual" restarts: if no improvement for X, perturb p by multiplying exp(noise*sigma), or smooth.

Need maybe use threshold target lower than current to try reduce to target. But if threshold too low, almost all coefficients active, direction may push to equal? Algorithm `_mmd_refine(p0, time, target, beta0, beta1, alpha0)`:
- Initialize p=sanitize(p0), best=p,score.
- Precompute L.
- Parameters: alpha=0.2 maybe. alphas = [alpha*0.25,0.5,1,2,4,8]? But α normalized h max1. If h max1, p changes by exp(-α). α up to 2. Grid can be [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8,1.2]. If no improvement, decrease.
- beta logsumexp exponent. Start 50, increase to 2000.
- eps threshold blend from 0 to 1.
- Main loop while time:
  - compute c, score.
  - if score<best update.
  - maxc=score/(2n), target_m=target/(2n). ratio=maxc/target_m.
  - if ratio >1.05: use threshold with tau = max(target_m, maxc*(1-eps)); if too close, use softmax.
  - else: eps>0.5 use softmax with beta.
  - h = corr(w,p) normalized.
  - Try line search. Maybe also try positive step? Since update exp(-αh) always decreases high h coordinates; but sometimes increasing high h and decreasing low h? Normalization forces sum, so if all high coefficients involve low coordinates? Direction should reduce max. Could also try `exp(+α h)`? Unlikely but for escaping maybe.
  - Choose best among score improvement. If none:
     - alpha *=0.8; counter++.
     - if counter large: random restart: multiply p by exp(normal*sigma) where sigma increases; accept if score not too worse (or worse up to anneal), then continue. Or reset to best.
  - Update beta, eps.
  - Periodically (e.g., no improve 100) try smoothing or coarsen-resample? Maybe.
- Return best.

The convolution h(s) is the area of intersection of two shifted supports weighted. If f is linear decreasing from left to right, maybe h flat? Let f(x)=A(1-x) on [0,1], ∫=A/2=1 => A=2. h(s)=∫0^s 2(1-x) 2(s-x) dx =4 ∫0^s (s-x)(1-x) dx. At s=1: 4∫0^1 (1-x)^2 dx=4/3=1.333. Not under1.

Search methods:
1. **Resample parent to multiple lengths** (1024,2048,4096,8192, maybe 16384) and quick evaluate.
2. **Generate analytical starts**:
   - normalized linear ramps increasing/decreasing maybe with endpoint offsets: p ∝ (A + B i + C i^2) for a few parameters (e.g., beta distributions, sine, cosine asymmetric).
   - The linear ramp f=0.5+x (A=0.5,B=1) continuous max 0.916, score 1.833, okay as start.
   - f = (1 + a * (2x/n-1)) for a in [0.5,0.8,1.0,1.2], normalized.
   - f = exponential/linear combinations: p ∝ exp(θ i/n) * (1+ε sin(2π i/n))? Maybe.
   - random smooth functions from previous modes.
   But parent likely best.

3. **Optimizer A: Multiplicative active mirror descent** (new with active worst-K uniform and ridge? maybe implement simple + adaptive). We'll call `_mmd_refine`.
   - Features:
     * Target score. Use `beta_scale` for softmax high: `beta = max(10, 1/(maxc)*beta_frac)` or `beta = beta_frac * n`.
     * Weights selection: Alternate between:

Key scoring:
```
def _fft_coeff(p, L=None):
    n=p.size; if L is None: L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    F=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(F*F,L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return c
def _score(p):
    c=_fft_coeff(p)
    return 2.0*p.size*c.max(), c
```

Pseudo for `_mmd_optimize`:
```
def _mmd_optimize(p0, time_limit, target=1.500, verbose=False):
    p=_sanitize(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    n=p.size; L=_conv_len(n)
    best=p.copy(); best_sc, best_c = _score_c(p,L)
    cur_sc=best_sc
    alpha=0.12
    beta_c=20.0
    topK = max(8, min(256, n//2048? n=8192 ->32?)) maybe fixed 32.
    it=0; stall=0; last_best=best_sc
    deadline=...
    while time<deadline:
        c = _score_c from p? To avoid recompute previous? We can recompute each iter for simplicity.
        sc, c = _score_c(p,L)
        if sc < best_sc: update
        maxc=sc/(2n)
        target_m=target/(2n)
        ratio=maxc/target_m
        # choose weights
        rmode = it % 4
        w=None
        if ratio > 1.30:
            K=max(8, min(128, n//128))  # for 8192 K=128? Maybe too wide. K=16.
            idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
            w=np.zeros(c.shape); w[idx]=1.0/K
        elif ratio > 1.05:
            tau=max(target_m, maxc*(1-0.15))
            viol=np.maximum(c-tau,0)
            if viol.sum()>0: w=viol/sum
            else: softmax
        else:
            # high beta softmax
            beta=beta_c/max(maxc,1e-300)
            w=np.exp(beta*(c-cmax)); w/=w.sum()
        h=_corr(p,w,L)  # zero-pad w length 2n-1
        h=np.maximum(h,0) # should
        scals=np.sqrt(np.dot(p,h*h))
        if small: random
        else: h/=scals
        # line search
        tests=[]; for mult in (0.25,0.5,1,2,4): a=alpha*mult...
        best_trial=None,bsc=sc
        for a in tests:
           try p2=p*np.exp(-a*h)
           normalize
           sc2,_ = _score_c(p2,L)
           if sc2 < bsc - 1e-12: update
           # Also if key? maybe not
        if best_trial:
           p=best_trial; sc=bsc; alpha=min(1.0, alpha*1.05); stall=0
        else:
           alpha=max(0.02, alpha*0.7)
           stall +=1
           if stall >=20: random perturb? But if we perturb and accept only if not terrible.
           if stall >=80: p=p*exp(noise sigma=0.1); normalize; sc,... if sc <= best_sc*1.05 accept else reset best
           if stall >=250: p=best.copy(); sc=best_sc; alpha=0.12; stall=0
        # adaptive parameters
        if it%20==0:
           beta_c=min(500, beta_c*1.1)
           # topK etc
        it+=1
    return best, best_sc
```

- Exponential: θ in [-2,-1,1,2]: p=exp(θ*x).
- Product of ramp and sine: p=(0.5+x)*(1+0.5*np.sin(2πx)) etc.

```
      if sc<curr: ...
```

`_mmd_optimize` details code:
```
def _mmd_optimize(p0, time_limit, target=1.500, alpha0=0.08, verbose=False):
    p=_sanitize(p0, _MIN_P, True)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    p=p - p.mean? no.
    n=int(p.size); L=_conv_len(n)
    deadline=time.monotonic()+float(time_limit)
    cur_sc, c = _score_c(p,L)
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=cur_sc
    alpha=float(alpha0); beta_c=20.0; it=0; stall=0; h_ema=None; last_print=-10000
    target_m = target/(2.0*n)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # ensure p normalized? maybe
       Fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
       c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,L)[:2*n-1]
       c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
       if c.size==0: break
       cur_sc = 2.0*n*float(c.max())
       if not np.isfinite(cur_sc): p=best_p; cur_sc=best_sc; continue
       if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
          best_sc=cur_sc; best_p=p.copy(); if verbose print...
       maxc=cur_sc/(2.0*n)
       ratio=maxc/target_m if target_m>0 else 1.0
       # weights
       K_full=2*n-1
       if ratio > 1.25:
          K=max(8, min(128, n//256? Use n*0.002))
       ...
```
Let's set K:
```
if ratio > 1.25:
   K = max(8, min(64, int(0.002*n)))
   mix=0.25; slack=0.08
elif ratio > 1.05:
   K = max(16, min(256, int(0.008*n)))
   mix=0.4; slack=0.2
else:
   K = max(32, min(1024, int(0.03*n)))
   mix=0.6; slack=0.5
```
For n=1024, 0.032n=32, okay. K < len(c). Use `K = min(K, 2*n-1)`.
Top weights:
```
if K <= 0 or K >= c.size: idx=np.arange(c.size); w_top=np.full(c.size,1.0/c.size)
else:
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
   w_top=np.zeros(c.size); w_top[idx]=1.0/K
```
Threshold:
```
tau=max(target_m, maxc*(1.0-slack))
if tau < maxc:
   viol=np.maximum(c-tau,0.0)
   sviol=violdot=np.sum(viol)
   if sviol>1e-300: w_viol=violdot/sviol
   else: w_viol=w_top
else: w_viol=w_top
```

```
def _make_analytic_starts(n, rng):
    x=(np.arange(n, dtype=float)+0.5)/float(n)
    starts=[]
    for a in (-0.8,-0.4,0.4,0.8,1.0,1.2):
        p=1.0 + a*x
        p=np.where(p>0,p,1e-6)
        p=np.maximum(0,p)+1e-12
        p/=p.sum()
        starts.append(p)
    for ap,bp in ((1.0,0.0),(0.0,1.0),(1.0,1.0),(0.4,0.0),(0.0,0.4),(0.8,0.5),(0.5,0.8),(2.0,0.0),(0.0,2.0)):
        tt=np.clip(x,1e-8,1-1e-8)
        p=np.power(tt,ap)*np.power(1-tt,bp)
        p=np.maximum(p,1e-12); p/=p.sum(); starts.append(p)
    for a,b in ((0.6,-0.2),(0.8,0.1),(-0.5,0.2),(0.3,0.5)):
        p=1 + a*np.sin(2*np.pi*x) + b*np.cos(2*np.pi*x)
        p=np.maximum(0.05,p); p/=p.sum(); starts.append(p)
    for th in (-2.0,-1.0,1.0,2.0):
        p=np.exp(th*x)
        p/=p.sum(); starts.append(p)
    # random smooth via rng maybe 2
    for _ in range(2): ...
    return starts
```
Add random modes similar previous: Gaussian bumps, piecewise constant. But starts many; score and top. Keep total maybe 20; okay.

Let's detail random smooth:
```
def _random_smooth_starts(n, rng, count=4):
  x=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
  out=[]
  for _ in range(count):
    mode=int(rng.integers(0,5))
    if mode==0:
       K=int(rng.integers(2,6))
       centers=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K))
       sigmas=rng.uniform(0.02,0.3,K)
       weights=rng.uniform(0.5,2,K)
       p=np.zeros(n)
       for ...: p += w*exp(-((x-c)/s)^2)
    elif mode==1:
       K=int(rng.integers(2,9))
       knots=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K+1))
       heights=rng.lognormal(0,1,K+1)
       p=np.interp(x, knots, heights)
    elif mode==2:
       K=int(rng.integers(4,33))
       widths=rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K)*0.7)
       heights=rng.lognormal(0,0.8,K)
       p=np.zeros(n); s=0
       for ii in range(K):
          e=int(round((s+widths[ii])*n)); p[s:e]=heights[ii]; s=e
    elif mode==3:
       a=float(rng.uniform(-0.9,1.3)); b=float(rng.uniform(-0.5,1.5))
       p=1+a*x+b*x*x; p=np.maximum(0.05,p)
    else:
       p=rng.lognormal(0,1,n); p=np.maximum(1e-6,p)
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0); p=np.maximum(1e-12,p); p/=p.sum(); out.append(p)
  return out
```
`rng.dirichlet` exists. Good.

```
def _crop_candidates(cands,p, sc=None, max_crops=10):
    p=_sanitize(p,0.0,True)
    if p is None: return
    n=p.size
    if n<64:
       _add_candidate(cands,p,sc)
       return
    fracs=[1.0,0.9,0.8,0.7,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.33,0.25,0.2]
    added=0
    for frac in fracs:
       m=int(round(n*frac))
       if m<128: continue
       if m>n: m=n
       starts=[0, (n-m)//2]
       # random two
       starts.append(int(_RNG.integers(0,n-m+1)))
       starts.append(int(_RNG.integers(0,n-m+1)))
       for st in starts:
          if added>=max_crops: break
          if st<0 or st+m>n: continue
          sub=p[st:st+m]
          sub_sc=_score(sub)
          if np.isfinite(sub_sc):
             _add_candidate(cands, sub, sub_sc, 32)
             added+=1
```

```
def _logmix(p1,p2,theta):
  a=_sanitize(p1,0,True); b=_sanitize(p2,0,True)
  if a is None or b is None: return None
  if a.size != b.size: b=_resample_linear(b,a.size)
  la=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-300)); lb=np.log(np.maximum(b,1e-300))
  z=(1-theta)*la+theta*lb
  p=np.exp(z - np.max(z)); p/=p.sum(); return p
```

```
def _block_polish(p0,time_limit,verbose=False):
 p=_sanitize(...); n; L; best; cur; deadline; stagnant=0; it=0
 factors=(0.7,0.85,0.95,1.05,1.2,1.4,0.5,1.6)? 8. Use 6 maybe.
 while time<deadline:
   r=_RNG.random()
   small=max(1,n//4096); mid=max(small+1,n//256); large=max(mid+1,min(n,n//32))
   if r<0.25: w=1
   elif r<0.55: w=int(_RNG.integers(small, max(small+1, small*4+1)))
   elif r<0.85: w=int(_RNG.integers(mid, max(mid+1, mid*4+1)))
   else: w=int(_RNG.integers(large, max(large+1, min(n,large*4)+1)))
   w=max(1,min(n,w)); start=int(_RNG.integers(0,n-w+1))
   # choose random block? maybe always random.
   best_cand=None; best_score=cur_sc
   # Maybe compute block mean to choose factor bias
   mblock=float(np.mean(p[start:start+w])) if w<n else 0
   if mblock > 1.0/n:
      factors=(0.5,0.7,0.85,0.95,1.05,1.2)
   elif mblock < 1.0/n:
      factors=(0.85,0.95,1.05,1.2,1.4,1.7)
   else:
      factors=(0.7,0.85,0.95,1.05,1.15,1.3)
   for f in factors:
      cand=p.copy(); cand[start:start+w] *= f; cand=np.maximum(cand,_MIN_P); s=cand.sum(); if valid cand/=s; sc,_=_score_c(cand,L); if sc<best_score-1e-12: ...
   if best_cand is not None: accept
   else:
      stagnant+=1
      if stagnant%20==0:
         sigma=0.05 + min(0.4,0.003*stagnant)
         p_try=_noise(p if stagnant<50 else best, sigma)
         sc_try,_=_score_c(p_try,L)
         if sc_try <= best_sc*1.03:
            p=p_try; cur_sc=sc_try; best maybe update
      if stagnant>150: p=best; cur=best_sc; stagnant=0
   it+=1
 return best,best_sc
```

`_conv_len`:
```
def _conv_len(n):
    n=int(max(1,n))
    if n in _CONV_CACHE: return _CONV_CACHE[n]
    need=2*n-1
    L=1
    while L<need: L <<=1
    _CONV_CACHE[n]=L
    return L
```

```
def _sanitize(p, min_p=0.0, normalize=True):
 if p is None: return None
 try: a=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()
 except: return None
 if a.size==0: return None
 a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
 if min_p>0: a=np.maximum(a,float(min_p))
 else: a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
 a=np.minimum(a,1000.0)
 if normalize:
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
   a=a/s
 return a
```

```
def _score_c(p, L=None):
    a=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()
    if a.size==0: return inf, np.array([])
    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return inf, np.array([])
    a=a/s
    n=a.size
    if L is None: L=_conv_len(n)
    Fp=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(Fp*Fp,L)
    c=c[:2*n-1]
    if c.size==0: ...
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    sc=2.0*n*float(c.max())
    return sc,c
```

```
def _to_unit_array(p):
    if p is None: return None
    try: a=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()

    a=np.where(np.isfinite(a),a,0.0)

    s=float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s<=0: return None
    return a/s
```

```
h_raw = _corr_w_p(w, Fp, L, n)
mean_h = float(np.dot(p,h_raw))
h_work = h_raw - mean_h
var = float(np.dot(p, h_work*h_work))
if var <= 1e-300: random continue
h_dir = h_work / np.sqrt(var)
if h_ema is None: h_ema = h_dir.copy()
else:
   h_ema = 0.7*h_ema + 0.3*h_dir
   h_dir = 0.5*h_dir + 0.5*h_ema
   mean_h = float(np.dot(p,h_dir)); h_dir -= mean_h
   var = float(np.dot(p,h_dir*h_dir))
   if var<=...: h_dir=0
   else: h_dir /= np.sqrt(var)
h_dir = np.clip(h_dir, -4.0, 4.0)
# renormalize? not needed. If all zeros after clip? no.
```

```python
"""
Multi-scale multiplicative active-coefficient search for non-negative sequences
minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)**2.

The code works with the convolution invariantly normalized to sum(a)=1.
It uses:
  - FFT-accelerated polynomial convolution scoring,
  - gradient / active-coefficient steering in the multiplicative (KL) mirror
    space,
  - centered directions,
  - annealed active-set width and log-sum-exp smoothing,
  - random log-perturbations and resets,
  - coarse-to-fine resampling,
  - stochastic block-scaling polish,
  - cropping to discover shorter sequences with better objective values.
"""

import numpy as np
import time

_GLOBAL_RNG = np.random.default_rng(12345)
_CONV_LEN_CACHE = {}
_MIN_OPT = 1e-300


def _candidate_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _scored_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _conv_len(n):
    n = int(max(1, n))
    cached = _CONV_LEN_CACHE.get(n)
    if cached is not None:
        return cached
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    _CONV_LEN_CACHE[n] = L
    return L


def _unit(p):
    """Normalize to a non-negative float vector of sum 1, or return None."""
    if p is None:
        return None
    try:
        a = np.asarray(p, dtype=float).ravel()
    except Exception:
        return None
    if a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return a / s


def _unit_floor(p, floor=0.0):
    """Like _unit, but force a tiny positive floor before normalizing."""
    a = _unit(p)
    if a is None:
        return None
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, float(floor))
        s = float(a.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
            return None
        a = a / s
    return a


def _score_c(p, L=None):
    """FFT score 2*n*max(conv(p,p)) assuming p is normalized (will normalize)."""
    a = _unit(p)
    if a is None:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=float)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=float)
    if L is None:
        L = _conv_len(n)
    Fp = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, L)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), np.array([], dtype=float)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return float("inf"), c
    return sc, c


def _score(p):
    sc, _ = _score_c(p)
    return sc


def _add_candidate(cands, p, sc=None, keep=32):
    a = _unit(p)
    if a is None:
        return
    if sc is None or not np.isfinite(sc):
        sc = _score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(sc):
        return
    cands.append((float(sc), int(a.size), a.copy()))
    cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    if len(cands) > keep:
        del cands[keep:]


def _resample_linear(p, m):
    a = _unit(p)
    if a is None:
        return None
    m = int(max(1, m))
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _unit_floor(out, _MIN_OPT)


def _resample_nearest(p, m):
    a = _unit(p)
    if a is None:
        return None
    m = int(max(1, m))
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float) / float(m)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    idx = np.rint((np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) * (float(n) / float(m)) - 0.5)
    idx = np.clip(idx, 0, n - 1).astype(int)
    out = a[idx]
    return _unit_floor(out, _MIN_OPT)


def _smooth_array(p, sigma):
    a = _unit_floor(p, _MIN_OPT)
    if a is None or sigma <= 0.0 or a.size < 5:
        return a
    sigma = float(sigma)
    rad = int(max(1, np.ceil(3.0 * sigma)))
    k = np.arange(-rad, rad + 1, dtype=float)
    kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (k / sigma) ** 2)
    s = float(kern.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return a
    kern = kern / s
    out = np.convolve(a, kern, mode="same")
    return _unit_floor(out, _MIN_OPT)


def _noise_array(p, sigma):
    a = _unit_floor(p, _MIN_OPT)
    if a is None or sigma <= 0.0:
        return a
    r = np.exp(float(sigma) * _GLOBAL_RNG.normal(0.0, 1.0, a.size))
    b = a * r
    return _unit_floor(b, _MIN_OPT)


def _log_mix(p1, p2, theta=0.5):
    a = _unit(p1)
    b = _unit(p2)
    if a is None or b is None:
        return None
    if a.size != b.size:
        b = _resample_linear(b, a.size)
        if b is None:
            return None
    la = np.log(np.maximum(a, _MIN_OPT))
    lb = np.log(np.maximum(b, _MIN_OPT))
    z = (1.0 - float(theta)) * la + float(theta) * lb
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    out = np.exp(z)
    return _unit_floor(out, _MIN_OPT)


def _random_smooth_starts(n, rng, count=4):
    n = int(max(4, n))
    out = []
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    for _ in range(int(count)):
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 5))
        if mode == 0:
            K = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
            centers = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
            sigmas = rng.uniform(0.02, 0.30, K)
            weights = rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0, K)
            p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            for ii in range(K):
                s = max(1e-6, float(sigmas[ii]))
                p = p + float(weights[ii]) * np.exp(-((x - float(centers[ii])) / s) ** 2)
        elif mode == 1:
            K = int(rng.integers(2, 9))
            knots = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K + 1))
            heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K + 1)
            p = np.interp(x, knots, heights)
        elif mode == 2:
            K = int(rng.integers(4, 33))
            widths = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K) * 0.7)
            heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 0.8, K)
            p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
            pos = 0
            for ii in range(K):
                end = int(round(pos + float(widths[ii]) * float(n)))
                end = min(n, max(pos + 1, end))
                p[pos:end] = float(heights[ii])
                pos = end
        elif mode == 3:
            a = float(rng.uniform(-0.8, 1.4))
            b = float(rng.uniform(-0.6, 1.6))
            p = 1.0 + a * x + b * x * x
        else:
            p = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
        p = np.maximum(p, 1e-12)
        out.append(_unit_floor(p, _MIN_OPT))
    return out


def _make_analytic_starts(n, rng):
    n = int(max(8, n))
    x = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    starts = []

    # Linear ramps, including increasing / decreasing off-center shapes.
    for a in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.4, 0.8, 1.0, 1.2):
        p = 1.0 + a * x
        p = np.where(p > 0.0, p, 0.02)
        starts.append(_unit_floor(p, _MIN_OPT))

    # Power-law shapes on [0,1].
    for ap, bp in (
        (1.0, 0.0),
        (0.0, 1.0),
        (1.0, 1.0),
        (0.4, 0.0),
        (0.0, 0.4),
        (0.8, 0.5),
        (0.5, 0.8),
        (2.0, 0.0),
        (0.0, 2.0),
    ):
        tt = np.clip(x, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
        p = np.power(tt, ap) * np.power(1.0 - tt, bp)
        starts.append(_unit_floor(p, _MIN_OPT))

    # Low-amplitude asymmetric Fourier shapes.
    for a, b in (
        (0.6, -0.2),
        (0.8, 0.1),
        (-0.5, 0.2),
        (0.3, 0.5),
    ):
        p = 1.0 + a * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x) + b * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        p = np.maximum(p, 0.05)
        starts.append(_unit_floor(p, _MIN_OPT))

    # Exponentials.
    for th in (-2.0, -1.0, 1.0, 2.0):
        p = np.exp(th * x)
        starts.append(_unit_floor(p, _MIN_OPT))

    # Random structured starts.
    for p in _random_smooth_starts(n, rng, 4):
        if p is not None:
            starts.append(p)

    return [s for s in starts if s is not None]


def _crop_candidates(cands, p, max_crops=12):
    a = _unit(p)
    if a is None:
        return
    n = int(a.size)
    if n < 128:
        _add_candidate(cands, a)
        return

    best_sc = float("inf")
    if cands:
        best_sc = min(item[0] for item in cands)

    fracs = (0.90, 0.80, 0.70, 0.60, 0.50, 0.40, 0.33, 0.25, 0.20)
    added = 0
    for frac in fracs:
        if added >= int(max_crops):
            break
        m = int(round(frac * float(n)))
        if m < 128 or m >= n:
            continue
        starts = (0, (n - m) // 2)
        starts = starts + (
            int(_GLOBAL_RNG.integers(0, n - m + 1)),
            int(_GLOBAL_RNG.integers(0, n - m + 1)),
        )
        for st in starts:
            if added >= int(max_crops):
                break
            st = int(max(0, min(n - m, st)))
            sub = a[st:st + m]
            sc = _score(sub)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc * 1.02 + 1e-9:
                _add_candidate(cands, sub, sc, 48)
                if sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = sc
                added += 1


def _mmd_optimize(p0, time_limit, target=1.495, alpha0=0.10, verbose=False):
    p = _unit_floor(p0, _MIN_OPT)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    n = int(p.size)
    L = _conv_len(n)

    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        sc, _ = _score_c(p, L)
        return p, sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + time_limit
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc, _ = _score_c(p, L)
    cur_sc = best_sc

    alpha = float(alpha0)
    beta_c = 30.0
    h_ema = None
    it = 0
    stall = 0
    last_print = -100000

    target_m = float(target) / (2.0 * float(n))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = _unit_floor(p, _MIN_OPT)
        if p is None:
            p = best_p.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc

        Fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(Fp * Fp, L)[:2 * n - 1]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        if c.size == 0:
            break
        cur_sc = 2.0 * float(n) * float(c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cur_sc):
            p = best_p.copy()
            cur_sc = best_sc
            continue

        if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = float(cur_sc)
            best_p = p.copy()
            if verbose and it - last_print >= 250:
                print(f"mmd n={n} it={it} best={best_sc:.7f}")
                last_print = it

        maxc = cur_sc / (2.0 * float(n))
        ratio = maxc / target_m if target_m > 0.0 else 1.5

        csize = c.size
        if ratio > 1.25:
            K = max(8, min(64, int(0.002 * float(n))))
            mix = 0.25
            slack = 0.08
        elif ratio > 1.05:
            K = max(16, min(256, int(0.008 * float(n))))
            mix = 0.40
            slack = 0.20
        else:
            K = max(32, min(1024, int(0.03 * float(n))))
            mix = 0.60
            slack = 0.50

        K = int(max(1, min(K, csize)))
        if K >= csize:
            w_top = np.full(csize, 1.0 / float(csize), dtype=float)
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(c, -K)[-K:]
            w_top = np.zeros(csize, dtype=float)
            w_top[idx] = 1.0 / float(K)

        if ratio < 1.02:
            beta = float(beta_c) / max(maxc, target_m, 1e-300)
            beta = min(1.0e5, max(1.0, beta))
            ex = np.clip(beta * (c - maxc), -60.0, 0.0)
            e = np.exp(ex)
            se = float(e.sum())
            if se > 0.0 and np.isfinite(se):
                w = e / se
            else:
                w = w_top
        else:
            tau = max(target_m, maxc * (1.0 - float(slack)))
            if tau < maxc:
                viol = np.maximum(c - tau, 0.0)
                sv = float(viol.sum())
                if sv > 1e-300:
                    w_viol = viol / sv
                else:
                    w_viol = w_top
            else:
                w_viol = w_top
            if mix < 1.0:
                w = (1.0 - float(mix)) * w_viol + float(mix) * w_top
            else:
                w = w_viol

        Fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
        h_raw = np.fft.irfft(Fw * np.conjugate(Fp), L)[:n]
        h_raw = np.maximum(h_raw, 0.0)

        mean_h = float(np.dot(p, h_raw))
        h_work = h_raw - mean_h
        var_h = float(np.dot(p, h_work * h_work))

        if not np.isfinite(var_h) or var_h <= 1e-300:
            p_try = _noise_array(p, 0.08)
            if p_try is not None:
                sc_try, _ = _score_c(p_try, L)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try <= max(best_sc * 1.03, cur_sc * 1.08):
                    p = p_try
                    cur_sc = sc_try
            stall += 1
            it += 1
            continue

        h_dir = h_work / np.sqrt(var_h)
        if h_ema is None:
            h_ema = h_dir.copy()
        else:
            h_ema = 0.70 * h_ema + 0.30 * h_dir
            h_dir = 0.50 * h_dir + 0.50 * h_ema
            mean_h2 = float(np.dot(p, h_dir))
            h_dir = h_dir - mean_h2
            var_h2 = float(np.dot(p, h_dir * h_dir))
            if np.isfinite(var_h2) and var_h2 > 1e-300:
                h_dir = h_dir / np.sqrt(var_h2)
            else:
                h_dir = h_work / np.sqrt(var_h)
        h_dir = np.clip(h_dir, -4.0, 4.0)

        trials = []
        for mult in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
            a = float(alpha) * float(mult)
            if a < 0.005:
                a = 0.005
            if a > 1.8:
                a = 1.8
            if (not trials) or abs(a - trials[-1]) > 1e-9:
                trials.append(a)

        best_trial = None
        best_trial_sc = cur_sc

        for a in trials:
            p_try = p * np.exp(-a * h_dir)
            p_try = np.maximum(p_try, _MIN_OPT)
            s_try = float(p_try.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s_try) or s_try <= 0.0:
                continue
            p_try = p_try / s_try
            sc_try, _ = _score_c(p_try, L)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_trial_sc - 1e-11:
                best_trial_sc = float(sc_try)
                best_trial = p_try

        if best_trial is not None:
            p = best_trial
            cur_sc = best_trial_sc
            alpha = min(1.6, float(alpha) * 1.04)
            stall = 0
        else:
            alpha = max(0.012, float(alpha) * 0.78)
            stall += 1

            if stall % 25 == 0:
                sigma = 0.05 + 0.003 * float(min(stall, 120))
                sigma = min(0.55, sigma)
                base = best_p if stall > 70 else p
                p_try = _noise_array(base, sigma)
                if p_try is not None:
                    sc_try, _ = _score_c(p_try, L)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try <= max(best_sc * 1.04, cur_sc * 1.12):
                        p = p_try
                        cur_sc = sc_try
                alpha = float(alpha0)
                h_ema = None
                stall = 0

            if stall > 260:
                p = best_p.copy()
                cur_sc = best_sc
                alpha = float(alpha0)
                h_ema = None
                stagger = beta_c
                beta_c = max(20.0, 0.5 * stagger)
                stall = 0

        if it % 18 == 0:
            if ratio < 1.06:
                beta_c = min(500.0, beta_c * 1.14)
            else:
                beta_c = max(12.0, beta_c * 0.94)

        it += 1

    return best_p, best_sc


def _block_polish(p0, time_limit, verbose=False):
    p = _unit_floor(p0, _MIN_OPT)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")

    time_limit = float(max(0.0, time_limit))
    n = int(p.size)
    L = _conv_len(n)
    if time_limit <= 0.0:
        sc, _ = _score_c(p, L)
        return p, sc

    deadline = time.monotonic() + time_limit
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc, _ = _score_c(p, L)
    cur_sc = best_sc
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -100000

    small = max(1, n // 4096)
    mid = max(small + 1, n // 256)
    large = max(mid + 1, min(n, n // 32))

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        r = float(_GLOBAL_RNG.random())
        if r < 0.25:
            w = 1
        elif r < 0.55:
            lo = small
            hi = min(n + 1, max(lo + 1, 4 * lo + 1))
            w = int(_GLOBAL_RNG.integers(lo, hi))
        elif r < 0.85:
            lo = mid
            hi = min(n + 1, max(lo + 1, 4 * lo + 1))
            w = int(_GLOBAL_RNG.integers(lo, hi))
        else:
            lo = large
            hi = min(n + 1, max(lo + 1, 4 * lo + 1))
            w = int(_GLOBAL_RNG.integers(lo, hi))

        w = int(max(1, min(n, w)))
        if w >= n:
            start = 0
        else:
            start = int(_GLOBAL_RNG.integers(0, n - w + 1))

        block = p[start:start + w]
        block_mean = float(block.mean()) if block.size else 0.0
        inv_n = 1.0 / float(n)

        if block_mean > 1.25 * inv_n:
            factors = (0.55, 0.70, 0.85, 0.95, 1.05, 1.15)
        elif block_mean < 0.8 * inv_n:
            factors = (0.85, 0.95, 1.05, 1.18, 1.35, 1.65)
        else:
            factors = (0.70, 0.85, 0.95, 1.05, 1.15, 1.30)

        try_p = None
        try_sc = cur_sc
        for f in factors:
            cand = p.copy()
            cand[start:start + w] *= float(f)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, _MIN_OPT)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
                continue
            cand = cand / s
            sc, _ = _score_c(cand, L)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < try_sc - 1e-12:
                try_sc = float(sc)
                try_p = cand

        if try_p is not None:
            p = try_p
            cur_sc = try_sc
            stagnant = 0
            if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best_sc = cur_sc
                best_p = p.copy()
                if verbose and it - last_print >= 250:
                    print(f"block n={n} it={it} best={best_sc:.7f}")
                    last_print = it
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant % 21 == 0:
                sigma = 0.05 + 0.0025 * float(min(stagnant, 140))
                sigma = min(0.45, sigma)
                base = best_p if stagnant > 60 else p
                p_try = _noise_array(base, sigma)
                if p_try is not None:
                    sc_try, _ = _score_c(p_try, L)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try <= max(best_sc * 1.03, cur_sc * 1.08):
                        p = p_try
                        cur_sc = sc_try
                        if cur_sc < best_sc:
                            best_sc = cur_sc
                            best_p = p.copy()
            if stagnant > 160:
                p = best_p.copy()
                cur_sc = best_sc
                stagnant = 0

        it += 1

    return best_p, best_sc


def _process_level(n, cands, parent, talloc, deadline, verbose=False):
    n = int(max(64, n))
    talloc = float(max(0.0, talloc))
    if talloc < 30.0 or float(deadline - time.monotonic()) < 50.0:
        return

    starts = []

    if parent is not None:
        sp_lin = _resample_linear(parent, n)
        if sp_lin is not None:
            starts.append(sp_lin)
        sp_near = _resample_nearest(parent, n)
        if sp_near is not None:
            starts.append(sp_near)

    best_cands = sorted(cands, key=_candidate_key)[:3]
    for item in best_cands:
        arr = item[2]
        if arr is None:
            continue
        if int(arr.size) != n:
            sp_lin = _resample_linear(arr, n)
            if sp_lin is not None:
                starts.append(sp_lin)
            sp_near = _resample_nearest(arr, n)
            if sp_near is not None:
                starts.append(sp_near)

    if len(cands) >= 2:
        sp_mix = _log_mix(cands[0][2], cands[1][2], 0.5)
        if sp_mix is not None and int(sp_mix.size) != n:
            sp_mix = _resample_linear(sp_mix, n)
        if sp_mix is not None:
            starts.append(sp_mix)

    for sp in _make_analytic_starts(n, _GLOBAL_RNG)[:5]:
        starts.append(sp)

    scored = []
    for sp in starts:
        sp = _unit(sp)
        if sp is None:
            continue
        sc = _score(sp)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), sp))
    if not scored:
        return

    scored.sort(key=_scored_key)

    if n >= 8192:
        keep = 2
    elif talloc > 350.0:
        keep = 3
    else:
        keep = 2

    if keep == 1:
        shares = (1.0,)
    elif keep == 2:
        shares = (0.65, 0.35)
    else:
        shares = (0.50, 0.35, 0.15)

    for idx in range(min(keep, len(scored))):
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem < 45.0:
            break
        t = float(shares[idx]) * talloc
        t = min(t, rem - 30.0)
        if t < 20.0:
            t = min(12.0, rem - 12.0)
        if t < 10.0:
            continue

        p, sc = _mmd_optimize(scored[idx][1], t, target=1.495, alpha0=0.10, verbose=verbose)
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 48)
            _crop_candidates(cands, p, max_crops=10)


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    global _GLOBAL_RNG

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    try:
        budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        budget = 2400.0
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget < 120.0:
        budget = 120.0

    total = max(90.0, float(budget) - 25.0)
    deadline = start_time + total

    try:
        iseed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        iseed = 12345
    _GLOBAL_RNG = np.random.default_rng((iseed % 1000000007) + 1)

    cands = []
    parent = None

    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        parent_raw = None
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None

    if parent_raw is not None:
        p = _unit(parent_raw)
        if p is not None:
            sc = _score(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 48)
                parent = p

    if not cands:
        p = np.ones(2048, dtype=float)
        p = p / p.sum()
        _add_candidate(cands, p, None, 48)
        parent = p

    verbose = total > 700.0

    parent_n = int(parent.size) if parent is not None else 3072

    if total > 1800.0:
        levels = [
            (1024, 0.020),
            (2048, 0.035),
            (3072, 0.095),
            (4096, 0.155),
            (8192, 0.455),
            (16384, 0.105),
        ]
    elif total > 900.0:
        levels = [
            (1024, 0.025),
            (2048, 0.050),
            (3072, 0.140),
            (4096, 0.285),
            (8192, 0.445),
        ]
    elif total > 400.0:
        levels = [
            (2048, 0.080),
            (3072, 0.300),
            (4096, 0.380),
            (8192, 0.175),
        ]
    else:
        levels = [
            (3072, 0.480),
            (4096, 0.340),
            (8192, 0.140),
        ]

    adjusted = []
    seen = set()
    for nn, ff in levels:
        nn = int(nn)
        if nn in seen:
            continue
        seen.add(nn)
        adjusted.append((nn, float(ff)))

    # If the supplied parent is at an unusual length, give it a shot.
    if parent_n not in seen and total > 500.0:
        adjusted.append((parent_n, 0.08))
        adjusted.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
        used = 0.0
        for nn, ff in adjusted:
            used += ff
        if used > 0.0:
            scale = max(0.1, 0.88 / used)
            adjusted = [(int(nn), float(ff) * scale) for nn, ff in adjusted]

    reserved = max(90.0, 0.07 * total)

    for nn, frac in adjusted:
        rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem <= reserved + 55.0:
            break
        talloc = frac * total
        talloc = min(talloc, rem - reserved - 25.0)
        if talloc < 18.0:
            continue
        _process_level(int(nn), cands, parent, talloc, deadline, verbose=verbose)
        if verbose and cands:
            print(f"after level {nn}, best={cands[0][0]:.7f}, len={cands[0][2].size}")

    if not cands:
        arr = np.ones(2048, dtype=float)
        return [float(x) / 2048.0 for x in arr]

    # Final focused polishing on the current global best.
    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 180.0:
        item = cands[0]
        t = min(rem - 45.0, 260.0 if item[2].size > 4096 else 200.0)
        if t > 35.0:
            p, sc = _mmd_optimize(item[2], t, target=1.49, alpha0=0.07, verbose=verbose)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 48)
                _crop_candidates(cands, p, max_crops=12)

    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 100.0:
        item = cands[0]
        t = min(rem - 35.0, 190.0)
        if t > 25.0:
            p, sc = _block_polish(item[2], t, verbose=verbose)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 48)
                _crop_candidates(cands, p, max_crops=12)

    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 90.0:
        item = cands[0]
        noisy = _noise_array(item[2], 0.18)
        if noisy is not None:
            t = min(rem - 35.0, 110.0)
            if t > 25.0:
                p, sc = _mmd_optimize(noisy, t, target=1.49, alpha0=0.09, verbose=verbose)
                if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                    _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 48)
                    _crop_candidates(cands, p, max_crops=12)

    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if rem > 60.0:
        item = cands[0]
        t = min(rem - 15.0, 85.0)
        if t > 12.0:
            p, sc = _mmd_optimize(item[2], t, target=1.49, alpha0=0.04, verbose=verbose)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 48)
                _crop_candidates(cands, p, max_crops=10)

    cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    best_item = cands[0]

    # Prefer a smaller length if it is almost as good, to avoid very slow
    # official O(n^2) evaluation.
    if best_item[2].size > 12000:
        for item in cands:
            if item[2].size <= 8192 and item[0] <= best_item[0] + 0.0015:
                best_item = item
                break

    selected = _unit(best_item[2])
    if selected is None:
        selected = _unit(best_item[2])
    if selected is None:
        selected = np.ones(2048, dtype=float) / 2048.0

    # Optional official spot-check when reasonably cheap.
    rem = float(deadline - time.monotonic())
    if selected.size <= 4096 and rem > 30.0:
        try:
            ev = evaluate_sequence
            for item in cands[:4]:
                arr = _unit(item[2])
                if arr is None or arr.size > 4096:
                    continue
                if float(deadline - time.monotonic()) < 8.0:
                    break
                lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
                sc = float(ev(lst))
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_item[0]:
                    best_item = item
                    selected = arr
        except NameError:
            pass
        except Exception:
            pass

    out = np.asarray(selected, dtype=float).ravel()
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        out = np.ones(2048, dtype=float)
        s = float(out.sum())
    else:
        out = out / s

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
